+// Counter-based sampling keeps every (i,j,ray) independent, which makes the
+// visibility work reproducible regardless of MPI rank or OpenMP schedule.
+inline uint32_t sampleBits(uint32_t x) {
+    x ^= x >> 16; x *= 0x7feb352dU; x ^= x >> 15;
+    x *= 0x846ca68bU; x ^= x >> 16; return x;
+}
+
+Vec3 randomPointInTriangleDeterministic(const Triangle& t, size_t i, size_t j, int ray) {
+    uint32_t base = static_cast<uint32_t>(i * 0x9e3779b9U + j * 0x85ebca6bU + ray * 0xc2b2ae35U);
+    val_t u = static_cast<val_t>(sampleBits(base)) / 4294967296.0f;
+    val_t v = static_cast<val_t>(sampleBits(base + 0x68bc21ebU)) / 4294967296.0f;
+    if (u + v > 1.0f) { u = 1.0f - u; v = 1.0f - v; }
+    return t.a + (t.b - t.a) * u + (t.c - t.a) * v;
+}
+

+// The accelerator kernels operate on a complete history.  MPI distributes
+// output rows; an allgather after each timestep supplies the next dependency.
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const Triangle* triangles, const val_t* areas,
+                                const val_t* rho, const val_t* kij, const int* tau,
+                                const val_t* radE, val_t* radB, size_t n, size_t t,
+                                size_t firstRow, size_t rows) {
+    size_t local = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= rows) return;
+    size_t i = firstRow + local;
+    val_t sumB = 0.0f;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        int delay = tau[i * n + j];
+        if (static_cast<int>(t) < delay) continue;
+        val_t factor = kij[i * n + j];
+        if (factor <= 0.0f) continue;
+        val_t source = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (source <= 0.0f) continue;
+        val_t weighted = factor * areas[j];
+        sumB += (weighted < 1.0f ? weighted : 1.0f) * source;
+    }
+    radB[t * n + i] = rho[i] * sumB + radE[t * n + i];
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* distances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t source,
+                               size_t firstRow, size_t rows) {
+    size_t local = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= rows) return;
+    size_t i = firstRow + local;
+    val_t maxCorr = 0.0f;
+    int bestT = 0;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < timesteps; ++t) {
+        val_t sum = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = t; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[ (tt - t) * n + source] * radB[tt * n + i];
+        if (sum > maxCorr) { maxCorr = sum; bestT = static_cast<int>(t); }
+    }
+    distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);
+}
+
+inline void cudaCheck(cudaError_t err, const char* what) {
+    if (err != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error in %s: %s\n", what, cudaGetErrorString(err));
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 2);
+    }
+}
+

+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&tau, s.tau.size()*sizeof(int)), "tau");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&radE, s.radE.size()*sizeof(val_t)), "radE");

+};

+// Counter-based sampling keeps every (i,j,ray) independent, which makes the
+// visibility work reproducible regardless of MPI rank or OpenMP schedule.
+inline uint32_t sampleBits(uint32_t x) {
+    x ^= x >> 16; x *= 0x7feb352dU; x ^= x >> 15;
+    x *= 0x846ca68bU; x ^= x >> 16; return x;
+}
+
+Vec3 randomPointInTriangleDeterministic(const Triangle& t, size_t i, size_t j, int ray) {
+    uint32_t base = static_cast<uint32_t>(i * 0x9e3779b9U + j * 0x85ebca6bU + ray * 0xc2b2ae35U);
+    val_t u = static_cast<val_t>(sampleBits(base)) / 4294967296.0f;
+    val_t v = static_cast<val_t>(sampleBits(base + 0x68bc21ebU)) / 4294967296.0f;
+    if (u + v > 1.0f) { u = 1.0f - u; v = 1.0f - v; }
+    return t.a + (t.b - t.a) * u + (t.c - t.a) * v;
+}
+

 // ============================================================================

+// The accelerator kernels operate on a complete history.  MPI distributes
+// output rows; an allgather after each timestep supplies the next dependency.
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const Triangle* triangles, const val_t* areas,
+                                const val_t* rho, const val_t* kij, const int* tau,
+                                const val_t* radE, val_t* radB, size_t n, size_t t,
+                                size_t firstRow, size_t rows) {
+    size_t local = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= rows) return;
+    size_t i = firstRow + local;
+    val_t sumB = 0.0f;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        int delay = tau[i * n + j];
+        if (static_cast<int>(t) < delay) continue;
+        val_t factor = kij[i * n + j];
+        if (factor <= 0.0f) continue;
+        val_t source = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (source <= 0.0f) continue;
+        val_t weighted = factor * areas[j];
+        sumB += (weighted < 1.0f ? weighted : 1.0f) * source;
+    }
+    radB[t * n + i] = rho[i] * sumB + radE[t * n + i];
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* distances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t source,
+                               size_t firstRow, size_t rows) {
+    size_t local = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= rows) return;
+    size_t i = firstRow + local;
+    val_t maxCorr = 0.0f;
+    int bestT = 0;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < timesteps; ++t) {
+        val_t sum = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = t; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[ (tt - t) * n + source] * radB[tt * n + i];
+        if (sum > maxCorr) { maxCorr = sum; bestT = static_cast<int>(t); }
+    }
+    distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);
+}
+
+inline void cudaCheck(cudaError_t err, const char* what) {
+    if (err != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error in %s: %s\n", what, cudaGetErrorString(err));
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 2);
+    }
+}
+
+struct CudaState {
+    Triangle *triangles = nullptr; val_t *areas = nullptr, *rho = nullptr;
+    val_t *kij = nullptr, *radE = nullptr, *radB = nullptr, *distances = nullptr;
+    int *tau = nullptr;
+    void allocate(const SimulationState& s) {
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&triangles, s.numTriangles*sizeof(Triangle)), "triangles");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&areas, s.numTriangles*sizeof(val_t)), "areas");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&rho, s.numTriangles*sizeof(val_t)), "rho");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&kij, s.kij.size()*sizeof(val_t)), "kij");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&tau, s.tau.size()*sizeof(int)), "tau");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&radE, s.radE.size()*sizeof(val_t)), "radE");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&radB, s.radB.size()*sizeof(val_t)), "radB");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&distances, s.distances.size()*sizeof(val_t)), "distances");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(triangles,s.triangles.data(),s.numTriangles*sizeof(Triangle),cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),"triangles copy");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(areas,s.areas.data(),s.numTriangles*sizeof(val_t),cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),"areas copy");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(rho,s.rho.data(),s.numTriangles*sizeof(val_t),cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),"rho copy");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(kij,s.kij.data(),s.kij.size()*sizeof(val_t),cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),"kij copy");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(tau,s.tau.data(),s.tau.size()*sizeof(int),cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),"tau copy");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(radE,s.radE.data(),s.radE.size()*sizeof(val_t),cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),"emission copy");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemset(radB,0,s.radB.size()*sizeof(val_t)),"radiosity clear");
+    }
+    ~CudaState() { cudaFree(triangles); cudaFree(areas); cudaFree(rho); cudaFree(kij); cudaFree(tau); cudaFree(radE); cudaFree(radB); cudaFree(distances); }
+};
+

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    int provided = 0;
    MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &provided);
    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &g_mpiRank);
    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &g_mpiSize);
    int deviceCount = 0;
    cudaCheck(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount), "device discovery");
    if (deviceCount == 0) {
        fprintf(stderr, "No CUDA device available on MPI rank %d\n", g_mpiRank);
        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 2);
    }
    cudaCheck(cudaSetDevice(g_mpiRank % deviceCount), "device selection");
    int targetTriangles = 320;
    int timesteps = 50;
    int sourceIdx = 0;
    val_t reflectivity = 0.8f;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;

    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            targetTriangles = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-t") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            timesteps = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-s") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            sourceIdx = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            reflectivity = static_cast<val_t>(atof(argv[++i]));
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-o") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            MPI_Finalize(); return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            MPI_Finalize(); return 1;
        }
    }

    int subdivisions = getSubdivisionsForTriangleCount(targetTriangles);

    printf("Room Response Simulation Benchmark\n");
    printf("===================================\n");
    printf("Target triangles: %d (using %d subdivisions)\n", targetTriangles, subdivisions);
    printf("Timesteps: %d\n", timesteps);
    printf("Source triangle: %d\n", sourceIdx);
    printf("Reflectivity: %.2f\n", reflectivity);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    printf("\n");

    // Initialize
    SimulationState state;
    initializeSimulation(state, subdivisions, static_cast<size_t>(timesteps),
                         static_cast<size_t>(sourceIdx), reflectivity);

    printf("\n");

    // Precomputation
    auto startPre = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();

    computeTimeDelays(state);
    computeFormFactors(state);

    std::vector<int> matrixCounts(g_mpiSize), matrixDispls(g_mpiSize);
    for (int r = 0; r < g_mpiSize; ++r) {
        size_t f = state.numTriangles * static_cast<size_t>(r) / g_mpiSize;
        size_t l = state.numTriangles * static_cast<size_t>(r + 1) / g_mpiSize;
        matrixCounts[r] = static_cast<int>((l - f) * state.numTriangles);
        matrixDispls[r] = static_cast<int>(f * state.numTriangles);
    }
    MPI_Allgatherv(state.tau.data() + ownedFirst(state.numTriangles) * state.numTriangles,
                   matrixCounts[g_mpiRank], MPI_INT, state.tau.data(), matrixCounts.data(),
                   matrixDispls.data(), MPI_INT, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    MPI_Allgatherv(state.kij.data() + ownedFirst(state.numTriangles) * state.numTriangles,
                   matrixCounts[g_mpiRank], MPI_FLOAT, state.kij.data(), matrixCounts.data(),
                   matrixDispls.data(), MPI_FLOAT, MPI_COMM_WORLD);

    auto endPre = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto preDuration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(endPre - startPre).count();

    printf("Precomputation time: %ld ms\n", preDuration);
    printf("\n");

    // Simulation
    auto startSim = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();

    runSimulation(state);

    auto endSim = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto simDuration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(endSim - startSim).count();

    printf("Simulation time: %ld ms\n", simDuration);
    printf("\n");

    // Distance computation
    auto startDist = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();

    computeDistances(state);

    auto endDist = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto distDuration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(endDist - startDist).count();

    printf("Distance computation time: %ld ms\n", distDuration);
    printf("\n");

    // Total time
    long totalTime = preDuration + simDuration + distDuration;
    printf("Total computation time: %ld ms\n", totalTime);

    // Performance metrics
    size_t n = state.numTriangles;
    size_t t = state.numTimesteps;
    double kijOps = static_cast<double>(n * n);
    double simOps = static_cast<double>(n * n * t);
    double distOps = static_cast<double>(n * t * t);

    printf("\nPerformance:\n");
    printf("  Triangles: %zu\n", n);
    printf("  Timesteps: %zu\n", t);
    printf("  Form factor computations: %.2e\n", kijOps);
    printf("  Simulation operations: %.2e\n", simOps);
    printf("  Distance computations: %.2e\n", distOps);
    printf("  Total time per triangle: %.4f ms\n", static_cast<double>(totalTime) / n);

    // Memory usage
    size_t memKij = n * n * sizeof(val_t);
    size_t memTau = n * n * sizeof(int);
    size_t memRad = 2 * t * n * sizeof(val_t);
    size_t totalMem = memKij + memTau + memRad;
    printf("  Memory usage: %.2f MB\n", totalMem / (1024.0 * 1024.0));

    // Hash
    uint64_t hash = computeHash(state);
    printf("  Result hash: %016lX\n", hash);
    printf("\n");
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {

+// Counter-based sampling keeps every (i,j,ray) independent, which makes the
+// visibility work reproducible regardless of MPI rank or OpenMP schedule.
+inline uint32_t sampleBits(uint32_t x) {
+    x ^= x >> 16; x *= 0x7feb352dU; x ^= x >> 15;
+    x *= 0x846ca68bU; x ^= x >> 16; return x;
+}
+
+Vec3 randomPointInTriangleDeterministic(const Triangle& t, size_t i, size_t j, int ray) {
+    uint32_t base = static_cast<uint32_t>(i * 0x9e3779b9U + j * 0x85ebca6bU + ray * 0xc2b2ae35U);
+    val_t u = static_cast<val_t>(sampleBits(base)) / 4294967296.0f;
+    val_t v = static_cast<val_t>(sampleBits(base + 0x68bc21ebU)) / 4294967296.0f;
+    if (u + v > 1.0f) { u = 1.0f - u; v = 1.0f - v; }
+    return t.a + (t.b - t.a) * u + (t.c - t.a) * v;
+}
+

+// The accelerator kernels operate on a complete history.  MPI distributes
+// output rows; an allgather after each timestep supplies the next dependency.
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const Triangle* triangles, const val_t* areas,
+                                const val_t* rho, const val_t* kij, const int* tau,
+                                const val_t* radE, val_t* radB, size_t n, size_t t,
+                                size_t firstRow, size_t rows) {
+    size_t local = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= rows) return;
+    size_t i = firstRow + local;
+    val_t sumB = 0.0f;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        int delay = tau[i * n + j];
+        if (static_cast<int>(t) < delay) continue;
+        val_t factor = kij[i * n + j];
+        if (factor <= 0.0f) continue;
+        val_t source = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (source <= 0.0f) continue;
+        val_t weighted = factor * areas[j];
+        sumB += (weighted < 1.0f ? weighted : 1.0f) * source;
+    }
+    radB[t * n + i] = rho[i] * sumB + radE[t * n + i];
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* distances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t source,
+                               size_t firstRow, size_t rows) {
+    size_t local = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= rows) return;
+    size_t i = firstRow + local;
+    val_t maxCorr = 0.0f;
+    int bestT = 0;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < timesteps; ++t) {
+        val_t sum = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = t; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[ (tt - t) * n + source] * radB[tt * n + i];
+        if (sum > maxCorr) { maxCorr = sum; bestT = static_cast<int>(t); }
+    }
+    distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);
+}
+
+inline void cudaCheck(cudaError_t err, const char* what) {
+    if (err != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error in %s: %s\n", what, cudaGetErrorString(err));
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 2);
+    }
+}
+
+struct CudaState {
+    Triangle *triangles = nullptr; val_t *areas = nullptr, *rho = nullptr;
+    val_t *kij = nullptr, *radE = nullptr, *radB = nullptr, *distances = nullptr;
+    int *tau = nullptr;
+    void allocate(const SimulationState& s) {
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&triangles, s.numTriangles*sizeof(Triangle)), "triangles");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&areas, s.numTriangles*sizeof(val_t)), "areas");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&rho, s.numTriangles*sizeof(val_t)), "rho");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&kij, s.kij.size()*sizeof(val_t)), "kij");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&tau, s.tau.size()*sizeof(int)), "tau");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&radE, s.radE.size()*sizeof(val_t)), "radE");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&radB, s.radB.size()*sizeof(val_t)), "radB");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&distances, s.distances.size()*sizeof(val_t)), "distances");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(triangles,s.triangles.data(),s.numTriangles*sizeof(Triangle),cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),"triangles copy");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(areas,s.areas.data(),s.numTriangles*sizeof(val_t),cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),"areas copy");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(rho,s.rho.data(),s.numTriangles*sizeof(val_t),cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),"rho copy");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(kij,s.kij.data(),s.kij.size()*sizeof(val_t),cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),"kij copy");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(tau,s.tau.data(),s.tau.size()*sizeof(int),cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),"tau copy");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(radE,s.radE.data(),s.radE.size()*sizeof(val_t),cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),"emission copy");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemset(radB,0,s.radB.size()*sizeof(val_t)),"radiosity clear");
+    }
+    ~CudaState() { cudaFree(triangles); cudaFree(areas); cudaFree(rho); cudaFree(kij); cudaFree(tau); cudaFree(radE); cudaFree(radB); cudaFree(distances); }
+};
+

+// Counter-based sampling keeps every (i,j,ray) independent, which makes the
+// visibility work reproducible regardless of MPI rank or OpenMP schedule.
+inline uint32_t sampleBits(uint32_t x) {
+    x ^= x >> 16; x *= 0x7feb352dU; x ^= x >> 15;
+    x *= 0x846ca68bU; x ^= x >> 16; return x;
+}
+
+Vec3 randomPointInTriangleDeterministic(const Triangle& t, size_t i, size_t j, int ray) {
+    uint32_t base = static_cast<uint32_t>(i * 0x9e3779b9U + j * 0x85ebca6bU + ray * 0xc2b2ae35U);
+    val_t u = static_cast<val_t>(sampleBits(base)) / 4294967296.0f;
+    val_t v = static_cast<val_t>(sampleBits(base + 0x68bc21ebU)) / 4294967296.0f;
+    if (u + v > 1.0f) { u = 1.0f - u; v = 1.0f - v; }
+    return t.a + (t.b - t.a) * u + (t.c - t.a) * v;
+}
+

 // ============================================================================

+// The accelerator kernels operate on a complete history.  MPI distributes
+// output rows; an allgather after each timestep supplies the next dependency.
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const Triangle* triangles, const val_t* areas,
+                                const val_t* rho, const val_t* kij, const int* tau,
+                                const val_t* radE, val_t* radB, size_t n, size_t t,
+                                size_t firstRow, size_t rows) {
+    size_t local = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= rows) return;
+    size_t i = firstRow + local;
+    val_t sumB = 0.0f;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        int delay = tau[i * n + j];
+        if (static_cast<int>(t) < delay) continue;
+        val_t factor = kij[i * n + j];
+        if (factor <= 0.0f) continue;
+        val_t source = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (source <= 0.0f) continue;
+        val_t weighted = factor * areas[j];
+        sumB += (weighted < 1.0f ? weighted : 1.0f) * source;
+    }
+    radB[t * n + i] = rho[i] * sumB + radE[t * n + i];
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* distances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t source,
+                               size_t firstRow, size_t rows) {
+    size_t local = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= rows) return;
+    size_t i = firstRow + local;
+    val_t maxCorr = 0.0f;
+    int bestT = 0;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < timesteps; ++t) {
+        val_t sum = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = t; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[ (tt - t) * n + source] * radB[tt * n + i];
+        if (sum > maxCorr) { maxCorr = sum; bestT = static_cast<int>(t); }
+    }
+    distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);
+}
+
+inline void cudaCheck(cudaError_t err, const char* what) {
+    if (err != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error in %s: %s\n", what, cudaGetErrorString(err));
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 2);
+    }
+}
+
+struct CudaState {
+    Triangle *triangles = nullptr; val_t *areas = nullptr, *rho = nullptr;
+    val_t *kij = nullptr, *radE = nullptr, *radB = nullptr, *distances = nullptr;
+    int *tau = nullptr;
+    void allocate(const SimulationState& s) {
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&triangles, s.numTriangles*sizeof(Triangle)), "triangles");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&areas, s.numTriangles*sizeof(val_t)), "areas");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&rho, s.numTriangles*sizeof(val_t)), "rho");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&kij, s.kij.size()*sizeof(val_t)), "kij");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&tau, s.tau.size()*sizeof(int)), "tau");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&radE, s.radE.size()*sizeof(val_t)), "radE");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&radB, s.radB.size()*sizeof(val_t)), "radB");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&distances, s.distances.size()*sizeof(val_t)), "distances");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(triangles,s.triangles.data(),s.numTriangles*sizeof(Triangle),cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),"triangles copy");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(areas,s.areas.data(),s.numTriangles*sizeof(val_t),cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),"areas copy");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(rho,s.rho.data(),s.numTriangles*sizeof(val_t),cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),"rho copy");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(kij,s.kij.data(),s.kij.size()*sizeof(val_t),cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),"kij copy");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(tau,s.tau.data(),s.tau.size()*sizeof(int),cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),"tau copy");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(radE,s.radE.data(),s.radE.size()*sizeof(val_t),cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),"emission copy");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemset(radB,0,s.radB.size()*sizeof(val_t)),"radiosity clear");
+    }
+    ~CudaState() { cudaFree(triangles); cudaFree(areas); cudaFree(rho); cudaFree(kij); cudaFree(tau); cudaFree(radE); cudaFree(radB); cudaFree(distances); }
+};
+

+// Counter-based sampling keeps every (i,j,ray) independent, which makes the
+// visibility work reproducible regardless of MPI rank or OpenMP schedule.
+inline uint32_t sampleBits(uint32_t x) {
+    x ^= x >> 16; x *= 0x7feb352dU; x ^= x >> 15;
+    x *= 0x846ca68bU; x ^= x >> 16; return x;
+}
+
+Vec3 randomPointInTriangleDeterministic(const Triangle& t, size_t i, size_t j, int ray) {
+    uint32_t base = static_cast<uint32_t>(i * 0x9e3779b9U + j * 0x85ebca6bU + ray * 0xc2b2ae35U);
+    val_t u = static_cast<val_t>(sampleBits(base)) / 4294967296.0f;
+    val_t v = static_cast<val_t>(sampleBits(base + 0x68bc21ebU)) / 4294967296.0f;
+    if (u + v > 1.0f) { u = 1.0f - u; v = 1.0f - v; }
+    return t.a + (t.b - t.a) * u + (t.c - t.a) * v;
+}
+

+// The accelerator kernels operate on a complete history.  MPI distributes
+// output rows; an allgather after each timestep supplies the next dependency.
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const Triangle* triangles, const val_t* areas,
+                                const val_t* rho, const val_t* kij, const int* tau,
+                                const val_t* radE, val_t* radB, size_t n, size_t t,
+                                size_t firstRow, size_t rows) {
+    size_t local = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= rows) return;
+    size_t i = firstRow + local;
+    val_t sumB = 0.0f;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        int delay = tau[i * n + j];
+        if (static_cast<int>(t) < delay) continue;
+        val_t factor = kij[i * n + j];
+        if (factor <= 0.0f) continue;
+        val_t source = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (source <= 0.0f) continue;
+        val_t weighted = factor * areas[j];
+        sumB += (weighted < 1.0f ? weighted : 1.0f) * source;
+    }
+    radB[t * n + i] = rho[i] * sumB + radE[t * n + i];
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* distances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t source,
+                               size_t firstRow, size_t rows) {
+    size_t local = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= rows) return;
+    size_t i = firstRow + local;
+    val_t maxCorr = 0.0f;
+    int bestT = 0;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < timesteps; ++t) {
+        val_t sum = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = t; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[ (tt - t) * n + source] * radB[tt * n + i];
+        if (sum > maxCorr) { maxCorr = sum; bestT = static_cast<int>(t); }
+    }
+    distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);
+}
+
+inline void cudaCheck(cudaError_t err, const char* what) {
+    if (err != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error in %s: %s\n", what, cudaGetErrorString(err));
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 2);
+    }
+}
+
+struct CudaState {
+    Triangle *triangles = nullptr; val_t *areas = nullptr, *rho = nullptr;
+    val_t *kij = nullptr, *radE = nullptr, *radB = nullptr, *distances = nullptr;
+    int *tau = nullptr;
+    void allocate(const SimulationState& s) {
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&triangles, s.numTriangles*sizeof(Triangle)), "triangles");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&areas, s.numTriangles*sizeof(val_t)), "areas");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&rho, s.numTriangles*sizeof(val_t)), "rho");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&kij, s.kij.size()*sizeof(val_t)), "kij");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&tau, s.tau.size()*sizeof(int)), "tau");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&radE, s.radE.size()*sizeof(val_t)), "radE");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&radB, s.radB.size()*sizeof(val_t)), "radB");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&distances, s.distances.size()*sizeof(val_t)), "distances");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(triangles,s.triangles.data(),s.numTriangles*sizeof(Triangle),cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),"triangles copy");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(areas,s.areas.data(),s.numTriangles*sizeof(val_t),cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),"areas copy");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(rho,s.rho.data(),s.numTriangles*sizeof(val_t),cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),"rho copy");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(kij,s.kij.data(),s.kij.size()*sizeof(val_t),cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),"kij copy");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(tau,s.tau.data(),s.tau.size()*sizeof(int),cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),"tau copy");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(radE,s.radE.data(),s.radE.size()*sizeof(val_t),cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),"emission copy");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemset(radB,0,s.radB.size()*sizeof(val_t)),"radiosity clear");
+    }
+    ~CudaState() { cudaFree(triangles); cudaFree(areas); cudaFree(rho); cudaFree(kij); cudaFree(tau); cudaFree(radE); cudaFree(radB); cudaFree(distances); }
+};
+

+// Counter-based sampling keeps every (i,j,ray) independent, which makes the
+// visibility work reproducible regardless of MPI rank or OpenMP schedule.
+inline uint32_t sampleBits(uint32_t x) {
+    x ^= x >> 16; x *= 0x7feb352dU; x ^= x >> 15;
+    x *= 0x846ca68bU; x ^= x >> 16; return x;
+}
+
+Vec3 randomPointInTriangleDeterministic(const Triangle& t, size_t i, size_t j, int ray) {
+    uint32_t base = static_cast<uint32_t>(i * 0x9e3779b9U + j * 0x85ebca6bU + ray * 0xc2b2ae35U);
+    val_t u = static_cast<val_t>(sampleBits(base)) / 4294967296.0f;
+    val_t v = static_cast<val_t>(sampleBits(base + 0x68bc21ebU)) / 4294967296.0f;
+    if (u + v > 1.0f) { u = 1.0f - u; v = 1.0f - v; }
+    return t.a + (t.b - t.a) * u + (t.c - t.a) * v;
+}
+

+// The accelerator kernels operate on a complete history.  MPI distributes
+// output rows; an allgather after each timestep supplies the next dependency.
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const Triangle* triangles, const val_t* areas,
+                                const val_t* rho, const val_t* kij, const int* tau,
+                                const val_t* radE, val_t* radB, size_t n, size_t t,
+                                size_t firstRow, size_t rows) {
+    size_t local = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= rows) return;
+    size_t i = firstRow + local;
+    val_t sumB = 0.0f;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        int delay = tau[i * n + j];
+        if (static_cast<int>(t) < delay) continue;
+        val_t factor = kij[i * n + j];
+        if (factor <= 0.0f) continue;
+        val_t source = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (source <= 0.0f) continue;
+        val_t weighted = factor * areas[j];
+        sumB += (weighted < 1.0f ? weighted : 1.0f) * source;
+    }
+    radB[t * n + i] = rho[i] * sumB + radE[t * n + i];
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* distances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t source,
+                               size_t firstRow, size_t rows) {
+    size_t local = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= rows) return;
+    size_t i = firstRow + local;
+    val_t maxCorr = 0.0f;
+    int bestT = 0;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < timesteps; ++t) {
+        val_t sum = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = t; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[ (tt - t) * n + source] * radB[tt * n + i];
+        if (sum > maxCorr) { maxCorr = sum; bestT = static_cast<int>(t); }
+    }
+    distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);
+}
+
+inline void cudaCheck(cudaError_t err, const char* what) {
+    if (err != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error in %s: %s\n", what, cudaGetErrorString(err));
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 2);
+    }
+}
+
+struct CudaState {
+    Triangle *triangles = nullptr; val_t *areas = nullptr, *rho = nullptr;
+    val_t *kij = nullptr, *radE = nullptr, *radB = nullptr, *distances = nullptr;
+    int *tau = nullptr;
+    void allocate(const SimulationState& s) {
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&triangles, s.numTriangles*sizeof(Triangle)), "triangles");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&areas, s.numTriangles*sizeof(val_t)), "areas");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&rho, s.numTriangles*sizeof(val_t)), "rho");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&kij, s.kij.size()*sizeof(val_t)), "kij");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&tau, s.tau.size()*sizeof(int)), "tau");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&radE, s.radE.size()*sizeof(val_t)), "radE");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&radB, s.radB.size()*sizeof(val_t)), "radB");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&distances, s.distances.size()*sizeof(val_t)), "distances");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(triangles,s.triangles.data(),s.numTriangles*sizeof(Triangle),cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),"triangles copy");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(areas,s.areas.data(),s.numTriangles*sizeof(val_t),cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),"areas copy");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(rho,s.rho.data(),s.numTriangles*sizeof(val_t),cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),"rho copy");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(kij,s.kij.data(),s.kij.size()*sizeof(val_t),cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),"kij copy");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(tau,s.tau.data(),s.tau.size()*sizeof(int),cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),"tau copy");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(radE,s.radE.data(),s.radE.size()*sizeof(val_t),cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),"emission copy");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemset(radB,0,s.radB.size()*sizeof(val_t)),"radiosity clear");
+    }
+    ~CudaState() { cudaFree(triangles); cudaFree(areas); cudaFree(rho); cudaFree(kij); cudaFree(tau); cudaFree(radE); cudaFree(radB); cudaFree(distances); }
+};
+

+// Counter-based sampling keeps every (i,j,ray) independent, which makes the
+// visibility work reproducible regardless of MPI rank or OpenMP schedule.
+inline uint32_t sampleBits(uint32_t x) {
+    x ^= x >> 16; x *= 0x7feb352dU; x ^= x >> 15;
+    x *= 0x846ca68bU; x ^= x >> 16; return x;
+}
+
+Vec3 randomPointInTriangleDeterministic(const Triangle& t, size_t i, size_t j, int ray) {
+    uint32_t base = static_cast<uint32_t>(i * 0x9e3779b9U + j * 0x85ebca6bU + ray * 0xc2b2ae35U);
+    val_t u = static_cast<val_t>(sampleBits(base)) / 4294967296.0f;
+    val_t v = static_cast<val_t>(sampleBits(base + 0x68bc21ebU)) / 4294967296.0f;
+    if (u + v > 1.0f) { u = 1.0f - u; v = 1.0f - v; }
+    return t.a + (t.b - t.a) * u + (t.c - t.a) * v;
+}
+

 // ============================================================================

+// The accelerator kernels operate on a complete history.  MPI distributes
+// output rows; an allgather after each timestep supplies the next dependency.
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const Triangle* triangles, const val_t* areas,
+                                const val_t* rho, const val_t* kij, const int* tau,
+                                const val_t* radE, val_t* radB, size_t n, size_t t,
+                                size_t firstRow, size_t rows) {
+    size_t local = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= rows) return;
+    size_t i = firstRow + local;
+    val_t sumB = 0.0f;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        int delay = tau[i * n + j];
+        if (static_cast<int>(t) < delay) continue;
+        val_t factor = kij[i * n + j];
+        if (factor <= 0.0f) continue;
+        val_t source = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (source <= 0.0f) continue;
+        val_t weighted = factor * areas[j];
+        sumB += (weighted < 1.0f ? weighted : 1.0f) * source;
+    }
+    radB[t * n + i] = rho[i] * sumB + radE[t * n + i];
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* distances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t source,
+                               size_t firstRow, size_t rows) {
+    size_t local = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= rows) return;
+    size_t i = firstRow + local;
+    val_t maxCorr = 0.0f;
+    int bestT = 0;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < timesteps; ++t) {
+        val_t sum = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = t; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[ (tt - t) * n + source] * radB[tt * n + i];
+        if (sum > maxCorr) { maxCorr = sum; bestT = static_cast<int>(t); }
+    }
+    distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);
+}
+
+inline void cudaCheck(cudaError_t err, const char* what) {
+    if (err != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error in %s: %s\n", what, cudaGetErrorString(err));
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 2);
+    }
+}
+
+struct CudaState {
+    Triangle *triangles = nullptr; val_t *areas = nullptr, *rho = nullptr;
+    val_t *kij = nullptr, *radE = nullptr, *radB = nullptr, *distances = nullptr;
+    int *tau = nullptr;
+    void allocate(const SimulationState& s) {
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&triangles, s.numTriangles*sizeof(Triangle)), "triangles");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&areas, s.numTriangles*sizeof(val_t)), "areas");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&rho, s.numTriangles*sizeof(val_t)), "rho");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&kij, s.kij.size()*sizeof(val_t)), "kij");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&tau, s.tau.size()*sizeof(int)), "tau");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&radE, s.radE.size()*sizeof(val_t)), "radE");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&radB, s.radB.size()*sizeof(val_t)), "radB");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&distances, s.distances.size()*sizeof(val_t)), "distances");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(triangles,s.triangles.data(),s.numTriangles*sizeof(Triangle),cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),"triangles copy");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(areas,s.areas.data(),s.numTriangles*sizeof(val_t),cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),"areas copy");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(rho,s.rho.data(),s.numTriangles*sizeof(val_t),cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),"rho copy");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(kij,s.kij.data(),s.kij.size()*sizeof(val_t),cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),"kij copy");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(tau,s.tau.data(),s.tau.size()*sizeof(int),cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),"tau copy");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(radE,s.radE.data(),s.radE.size()*sizeof(val_t),cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),"emission copy");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemset(radB,0,s.radB.size()*sizeof(val_t)),"radiosity clear");
+    }
+    ~CudaState() { cudaFree(triangles); cudaFree(areas); cudaFree(rho); cudaFree(kij); cudaFree(tau); cudaFree(radE); cudaFree(radB); cudaFree(distances); }
+};
+

codex
Implemented the hybrid MPI/OpenMP/CUDA roomsim version.

Changes:

- MPI row decomposition for form factors, delays, radiosity, and distances.
- OpenMP parallelization for CPU precomputation.
- CUDA kernels for wave propagation and cross-correlation.
- MPI synchronization of distributed radiosity rows each timestep.
- Deterministic parallel ray sampling.
- CMake updated to require CUDA, MPI, and OpenMP while preserving executable name `roomsim`.

Modified files:

- [roomsim.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/roomsim_gpt-5.6-luna-medium_hybrid_r4/roomsim/roomsim.cpp)
- [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/roomsim_gpt-5.6-luna-medium_hybrid_r4/roomsim/CMakeLists.txt)

The project builds successfully, and single-rank CUDA execution with validation passed. Multi-rank execution encountered a CUDA driver/runtime segmentation fault in the test environment during concurrent GPU initialization.

+// Counter-based sampling keeps every (i,j,ray) independent, which makes the
+// visibility work reproducible regardless of MPI rank or OpenMP schedule.
+inline uint32_t sampleBits(uint32_t x) {
+    x ^= x >> 16; x *= 0x7feb352dU; x ^= x >> 15;
+    x *= 0x846ca68bU; x ^= x >> 16; return x;
+}
+
+Vec3 randomPointInTriangleDeterministic(const Triangle& t, size_t i, size_t j, int ray) {
+    uint32_t base = static_cast<uint32_t>(i * 0x9e3779b9U + j * 0x85ebca6bU + ray * 0xc2b2ae35U);
+    val_t u = static_cast<val_t>(sampleBits(base)) / 4294967296.0f;
+    val_t v = static_cast<val_t>(sampleBits(base + 0x68bc21ebU)) / 4294967296.0f;
+    if (u + v > 1.0f) { u = 1.0f - u; v = 1.0f - v; }
+    return t.a + (t.b - t.a) * u + (t.c - t.a) * v;
+}
+

+// The accelerator kernels operate on a complete history.  MPI distributes
+// output rows; an allgather after each timestep supplies the next dependency.
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const Triangle* triangles, const val_t* areas,
+                                const val_t* rho, const val_t* kij, const int* tau,
+                                const val_t* radE, val_t* radB, size_t n, size_t t,
+                                size_t firstRow, size_t rows) {
+    size_t local = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= rows) return;
+    size_t i = firstRow + local;
+    val_t sumB = 0.0f;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        int delay = tau[i * n + j];
+        if (static_cast<int>(t) < delay) continue;
+        val_t factor = kij[i * n + j];
+        if (factor <= 0.0f) continue;
+        val_t source = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (source <= 0.0f) continue;
+        val_t weighted = factor * areas[j];
+        sumB += (weighted < 1.0f ? weighted : 1.0f) * source;
+    }
+    radB[t * n + i] = rho[i] * sumB + radE[t * n + i];
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* distances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t source,
+                               size_t firstRow, size_t rows) {
+    size_t local = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= rows) return;
+    size_t i = firstRow + local;
+    val_t maxCorr = 0.0f;
+    int bestT = 0;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < timesteps; ++t) {
+        val_t sum = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = t; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[ (tt - t) * n + source] * radB[tt * n + i];
+        if (sum > maxCorr) { maxCorr = sum; bestT = static_cast<int>(t); }
+    }
+    distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);
+}
+
+inline void cudaCheck(cudaError_t err, const char* what) {
+    if (err != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error in %s: %s\n", what, cudaGetErrorString(err));
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 2);
+    }
+}
+
+struct CudaState {
+    Triangle *triangles = nullptr; val_t *areas = nullptr, *rho = nullptr;
+    val_t *kij = nullptr, *radE = nullptr, *radB = nullptr, *distances = nullptr;
+    int *tau = nullptr;
+    void allocate(const SimulationState& s) {
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&triangles, s.numTriangles*sizeof(Triangle)), "triangles");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&areas, s.numTriangles*sizeof(val_t)), "areas");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&rho, s.numTriangles*sizeof(val_t)), "rho");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&kij, s.kij.size()*sizeof(val_t)), "kij");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&tau, s.tau.size()*sizeof(int)), "tau");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&radE, s.radE.size()*sizeof(val_t)), "radE");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&radB, s.radB.size()*sizeof(val_t)), "radB");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&distances, s.distances.size()*sizeof(val_t)), "distances");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(triangles,s.triangles.data(),s.numTriangles*sizeof(Triangle),cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),"triangles copy");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(areas,s.areas.data(),s.numTriangles*sizeof(val_t),cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),"areas copy");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(rho,s.rho.data(),s.numTriangles*sizeof(val_t),cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),"rho copy");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(kij,s.kij.data(),s.kij.size()*sizeof(val_t),cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),"kij copy");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(tau,s.tau.data(),s.tau.size()*sizeof(int),cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),"tau copy");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(radE,s.radE.data(),s.radE.size()*sizeof(val_t),cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),"emission copy");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemset(radB,0,s.radB.size()*sizeof(val_t)),"radiosity clear");
+    }
+    ~CudaState() { cudaFree(triangles); cudaFree(areas); cudaFree(rho); cudaFree(kij); cudaFree(tau); cudaFree(radE); cudaFree(radB); cudaFree(distances); }
+};
+

75,583
Implemented the hybrid MPI/OpenMP/CUDA roomsim version.

Changes:

- MPI row decomposition for form factors, delays, radiosity, and distances.
- OpenMP parallelization for CPU precomputation.
- CUDA kernels for wave propagation and cross-correlation.
- MPI synchronization of distributed radiosity rows each timestep.
- Deterministic parallel ray sampling.
- CMake updated to require CUDA, MPI, and OpenMP while preserving executable name `roomsim`.

Modified files:
